- **continuity-editor**: A continuity checker that reads a manuscript against its own established facts, world rules, and timeline and flags contradictions — eyes that change colour, a dead character who reappears, a Tuesday that can't exist, a secret someone acts on before they learn it. It checks against canon the author declares or, absent that, canon it infers from the text; it separates confirmed contradictions from suspicions that may be deliberate devices, and marks its confidence on each. Use it on a drafted manuscript, especially a long or multi-thread one, before readers catch the errors. It flags the colli… ([Calliope-Editor/writing-skills](https://github.com/Calliope-Editor/writing-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/editorial/continuity-editor) · ★4)
